[\[1a.1\]]@#/_/'dul ba ga bzhugs so/_/
[\[1b\]]
[\[1b.1\]]'dul ba gzhi/_bam po lnga bcu lnga pa/_thun mong ma yin pa gang zhe na/_dge slong dag sngon byung ba bA rA Na sI'i grong khyer du rgyal po tshangs sbyin zhes bya 
[\[1b.2\]]ba rgyal srid byed du 'jug ste 'byor pa dang zhes rgyas par bya ste/_de'i tshe smad 'tshong ma bzang mo zhes bya ba dang /_skyes bu g.yon can pad ma'i rtsa lag ces bya ba zhig gnas so/_/
[\[1b.3\]]des de la dga' mgur spyad pa'i phyir gos dang /_rgyan rnams bskur ba dang /_des gos dang rgyan rnams bgos nas song ba las/_skyes pa gzhan zhig kyang dong tshe lnga brgya 
[\[1b.4\]]thogs te 'ongs nas/_bzang mo dga' mgur spyad kyis tshur shog ces smras so/_/des bsams pa/_gal te song na ni dong tshe lnga brgya yang mi 'thob la/_khyim du 
[\[1b.5\]]'ongs pa bor te gzhan du 'gro ba 'di ni gsol ka mi yang bar yang 'gyur ro snyam nas/_des bu mo mngag gzhug ma la smras pa/_song la pad ma'i rtsa lag la jo bo re zhig 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]bdag gis sta gon ma bgyis kyis slad kyis mchi'o zhes zer ro zhes smros shig/_de de'i gan du song nas smras so/_/skyes pa de yang bya ba mang po zhig pas dga' mgur spyad nas srod nyid la phyir song ngo /_/des 
[\[2a.2\]]bsams pa/_thun mong du yod pas bdag gis de'i sems kyang bsrung nus so snyam nas/_des yang bu mo de la smras pa/_song la pad ma'i rtsa lag la jo bo sta gon bgyis lags na/_skyed mos tshal gang du mchi shig ces mchi'o 
[\[2a.3\]]zhes smros shig_/des de'i drung du song nas smras pa dang /_des smras pa/_khyod kyi jo mo skad cig gis ni sta gon byas/_skad cig gis ni sta gon ma byas/_bu mo de yang de dang rigs mi 'phrod pa zhig pas des smras pa/_rje'i sras des 
[\[2a.4\]]sta gon ma bgyis te/_'on kyang khyod kyi gos dang rgyan rnams kyis skyes pa gzhan dang lhan cig dga' mgur spyad do/_/de 'dod chags kyi kun nas dkris pa gang yin pa de dang ni bral/_gnod sems kyis kun nas dkris pa ni skyes par gyur te/_
[\[2a.5\]]de mi bzod pa skyes nas smras pa/_bu mo bzang mo'i drung du song la/_pad ma'i rtsa lag na re skyed mos tshal ga ge mo zhig tu shog shig ces zer ro zhes smros shig_/des song nas bzang mo la smras pa dang /_de nas de skyed mos tshal der song ba dang /_
[\[2a.6\]]skyes bu g.yon can pad ma'i rtsa lag gis smras pa/_khyod kho bo'i gos dang rgyan gyis gzhan dang lhan cig dga' mgur yongs su spyad pa ji ga rigs sam des smras pa/_rje'i sras 'di ni bdag gis nongs lags te/_'on kyang bud med ni 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]rtag par skyon can lags kyis bzod pa mdzod cig_/de nas des mi bzod pa skyes nas/_ral gri shubs nas phyung ste srog dang phral lo/_/de nas bu mo des jo mo bsad do jo mo bsad do zhes ku co'i sgra chen po bton pa de skye bo'i tshogs chen pos thos nas 
[\[2b.2\]]brgyugs so/_/de tsam na skyed mos tshal de na rang sangs rgyas legs smon zhes bya ba bsam gtan byed do/_/de nas skyes bu g.yon can pad ma'i rtsa lag de skrag pas ral gri khrag can rang sangs rgyas legs smon gyi mdun du bor nas skye bo'i tshogs chen 
[\[2b.3\]]po de nyid kyi nang du zhugs so/_/skye bo'i tshogs chen pos ral gri khrag can mthong nas rab tu byung ba 'dis ni bzang mo'i srog dang phral lo zhes de nas rang sangs rgyas de kun nas bskor nas mi bzod pa bskyed de smras pa/_kye rab tu byung ba drang srong gi rgyal mtshan 
[\[2b.4\]]bsrel nas 'di 'dra ba'i las byed dam/_des smras pa ci zhig byas/_de rnams kyis smras pa/_khyod kyis bzang mo dang lhan cig dga' mgur spyad nas de srog dang phral lo/_/des smras pa/_rnas mi bzod pa de nga ni las 'di mi byed do/_/des 
[\[2b.5\]]rnas mi bzod pa'i tshig smras kyang /_skye bo'i tshogs chen po des lag pa phying ka log tu dam du bcings nas rgyal po la phul te/_lha rab tu byung ba 'dis bzang mo dang lhan cig dga' mgur spyad nas des srog dang phral lags so/_/rgyal po dag ni mi 
[\[2b.6\]]rtog pa yin pas des smras pa/_gal te de lta na rab tu byung ba 'di ngas spangs kyis deng la sod cig_/de nas de'i mgul par me tog ka ra bI ra'i phreng bas btags nas gshed ma gos sngon po gyon pa mtshon thogs pa rnams kyis yongs su bskor nas lam 
[\[2b.7\]]po che dang /[ (]tshos [,]tshong [)]dus dang /_bzhi mdo dang /_sum mdo rnams su bsgrags te/_grong khyer nas skyed mos tshal logs su khrid do/_/de nas skyes bu g.yon can pad ma'i rtsa lag gi blo skyes pa rab tu byung ba dka' thub can skyon med pa nyes pa mi byed pa 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]'di la yang dag pa ma yin pas skur ba btab pas da 'di gsad pa la thug na/_bdag gis 'di yal bar dor ba ni bdag gi cha ma yin no snyam du bsams nas slar log ste/_rgyal po'i drung du song nas rkang pa gnyis la gtugs te smras 
[\[3a.2\]]pa/_lha rab tu byung ba 'dis ni las 'di ma bgyis te/_sdig pa'i las 'di ni bdag gis bgyis kyis rab tu byung ba 'di gtang bar gsol/_dge slong dag ji snyam du sems/_de'i tshe de'i dus kyi skyes bu g.yon can pad ma'i rtsa lag gang yin pa 
[\[3a.3\]]de ni nga nyid yin te/_ngas rang sangs rgyas la yang dag par ma yin pas skur pa btab pa gang yin pa de'i las kyi rnam par smin pas nga lo mang po dang zhes bya ba nas/_sems can dmyal ba rnams su btsos so zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/
[\[3a.4\]]las kyi lhag ma de kho nas da ltar nga mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa na yang bram ze'i bu mo rtswa mis yang dag pa ma yin pas skur pa btab ste/_'di ni thun mong ma yin pa'o/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyis phrin las ci zhig mdzad na/_phrin las de'i 
[\[3a.5\]]rnam par smin pas yul dgra mthar dge slong gnyis kyis lnga brgyar ma tshang ba'i dge slong rnams dang thabs cig nas rul gsol la/_tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu dang /_maud gal gyi bu gnyis ni lha'i zhal zas la longs spyod lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis 
[\[3a.6\]]bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag de bzhin gshegs pa nyid kyis sngon gyi tshe rabs gzhan rnams su las rnams byas shing bsags pa zhes bya ba nas/_'bras bu dag tu 'gyur ba nyid ces bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/dge slong dag sngon byung ba skye 
[\[3a.7\]]dgu rnams kyi tshe lo brgyad khri thub pa na 'jig rten du ston pa rnam par gzigs zhes bya ba zhig byung nas zhes bya ba nas/_sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/de 'khor drug khri dang ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs pa na/_rgyal po'i pho brang 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]ba shes ldan du byon to/_/bshes ldan de'i bram ze zhig bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya la bram ze rnams kyi gsang tshig klog tu 'jug go/_/des yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnam par gzigs 'khor lnga brgya dang mthong nas/_des bram ze'i khye'u rnams la 
[\[3b.2\]]smras pa/_shes ldan dag dge sbyong mgo reg 'di dag lha'i zhal zas la longs spyad pa'i 'os ma yin gyi/_nas rul pa za ba'i 'os yin no/_/de rnams kyis kyang rjes su yi rang ba byas te/_mkhan po de mad de/_dge sbyong mgo reg 'di dag ni lha'i zhal zas 
[\[3b.3\]]la longs spyad pa'i 'os ma lags kyi nas rul pa 'tshal ba'i 'os lags so/_/de na yang bram ze'i khye'u dkar po can gnyis shig gis smras pa/_mkhan pos ni ma legs pa zhig gsungs te/_bdag nyid chen po 'di dag ni lha'i zhal zas la longs spyod pa'i 'os 
[\[3b.4\]]lags so/_/dge slong dag ji snyam du sems/_de'i tshe de'i dus kyi bram ze lnga brgya can gang yin pa de ni nga nyid yin te/_ngas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnam par gzigs nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa la ngag gi las rtsub mo phyung ba gang yin pa 
[\[3b.5\]]de'i las kyi rnam par smin pas nga lo mang po dang /_lo brgya phrag mang po dang /_lo stong phrag mang po dang /_lo brgya stong phrag mang por nas rul pa zos so/_/las kyi lhag ma de kho nas de ltar mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa na yang dgra mthar dge slong 
[\[3b.6\]]gnyis kyis lnga brgyar ma tshang ba'i dge slong rnams dang thabs cig nas rul pa la longs spyad do/_/bram ze'i khye'u gnyis po gang yin pa de ni dge slong shA ri'i bu dang /_maud gal gyi bu 'di dag nyid yin no/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyis phrin las ci zhig 
[\[3b.7\]]mdzad na phrin las de'i rnam par smin pas lo drug tu dka' ba spyad par gyur lags/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag de bzhin gshegs pa nyid kyis sngon gyi tshe rabs gzhan rnams su las rnams byas shing bsags pa dag ni zhes bya ba nas/_
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]'bras bu dag tu 'gyur ba nyid ces bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/dge slong dag sngon byung ba ljongs kyi grong rdal bai b+hi Ting gi zhes bya ba 'byor pa dang rgyas pa/_bde ba dang /_lo legs pa/_skye bo dang /_mi mang pos rab tu gang ba 
[\[4a.2\]]zhig yod de/_dge slong dag ljongs kyi grong rdal bai b+hi Ting gi na rdza mkhan dga' skyong zhes bya ba sangs rgyas la mngon par dad pa/_chos dang dge 'dun la mngon par dad pa/_sangs rgyas la skyabs su song ba/_chos dang dge 'dun la skyabs su song ba/_
[\[4a.3\]]sangs rgyas la gcig tu byed pa/_chos dang dge 'dun la gcig tu byed pa/_sangs rgyas la yid gnyis dang the tshom med pa/_chos dang dge 'dun la yid gnyis dang the tshom med pa/_sdug bsngal dang kun 'byung dang /_'gog pa dang /_lam la 
[\[4a.4\]]yid gnyis dang the tshom med pa/_bden pa mthong ba/_'bras bu thob pa/_lo ma dang /_gtun spangs pa zhig byung ste/_de gang na chus brkos pa'am/_byi brun ma gtogs pa rang gi lag gis sa mi rko zhing rkor mi 'jug go/_/de nas rang gis sa 
[\[4a.5\]]blangs nas srog chags med pa'i chus sprus te snod rnams byas nas mtha' gcig tu bzhag ste/_'di skad ces 'phags pa'am/_bzhin [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag 'dir til lam/_'bras sam/_sran mud ga tshal tshol dag mtha' gcig tu zhog la/_khyed cag 
[\[4a.6\]]las gang zhig snod gang dgos pa de de long la deng shig ces smras nas/_de nas pha ma long ba gnyis gso zhing dus dus su yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsod snyoms 'bul lo/_/bram ze'i khye'u bla ma'i grogs po dang /_snying du sdug pa dang /_
[\[4a.7\]]ne'u zla yang yin no/_/dge slong dag ljongs kyi grong khyer bai b+hi Ting gi na bram ze shing sA la chen po lta bu n+ya gro d+ha zhes bya ba phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che ba zhig byung ste/_bai b+hi Ting gi rgyas pa/_'du thang dang bcas rtswa shing dang 'bab chur bcas pa/_rgyal 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]po kr-i kIs bram ze'i sbyin par byin pa la yongs su longs spyod do/_/bram ze shing sA la chen po lta bu n+ya gro d+ha'i slob ma bram ze'i khye'u bla ma zhes bya ba pha dang ma'i rigs dang ldan pa/_mo phyogs nas dag pa/_mes po'i phyogs dang /_phyi mo'i phyogs zung 
[\[4b.2\]]bdun rgyud kyi bar gyis bzung ste/_rigs la gtam gyis ma phog pa/_klog tu 'jug pa/_gsang tshig 'dzin pa/_rig byed gsum po sgra nges par sbyar ba dang /_shes gsal dang bcas pa'i yi ge rab tu 'byed pa dang bcas pa dang /_lnga pa de 
[\[4b.3\]]lta bu byung ba can rnams kyis pha rol tu phyin cing lung ston par byed pa/_gzugs bzang ba/_blta na sdug pa/_mdzes pa zhig yod de/_bram ze shing sA la chen po lta bu n+ya gro d+ha'i bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya tsam la bram ze rnams kyi gsang tshig klog tu 'jug go/_/
[\[4b.4\]]rdza mkhan dga' skyong gi grogs po dang /_snying du sdug pa dang ne'u zla yang yin no/_/de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi zhabs gnyis 
[\[4b.5\]]la mgo bos phyag byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gis rdza mkhan dga' skyong la chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par ston par mdzad/_yang dag par 'dzin du 
[\[4b.6\]]'jug par mdzad/_yang dag par gzengs stod par mdzad/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad de/_rnam grangs du mar chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par bstan/_yang dag par 'dzin du bcug_/yang dag par gzengs bstod/_yang dag 
[\[4b.7\]]par rab tu dga' bar mdzad nas cang mi gsung ngo /_/de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gis gsungs pa la mngon par dga' ste rjes su yi rangs nas/_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te stan 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]las langs nas song ngo /_/de'i tshe bram ze'i khye'u bla ma rta rgod ma dang ldan pa'i shing rta thams cad nas dkar ba'i nang du zhugs te/_gser gyi ril ba dang /_dbyug pa thogs nas bram ze'i khye'u'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor cing /_bram ze'i 
[\[5a.2\]]khye'u'i tshogs kyis mdun du bdar nas/_bai b+hi Ting gi phyi rol du bram ze rnams klog tu 'jug par 'dod nas/_bai b+hi Ting gi nas byung ba dang bram ze'i khye'u bla mas rdza mkhan dga' skyong thag ring po kho na nas mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di skad ces smras so/_/
[\[5a.3\]]des pa dga' skyong khyod da gzod ga las 'ongs/_des pa bla ma kho bo ni 'o ni nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen bkur byed du 'gros des pa bla ma tshur shog_/bdag cag yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen bkur byed du 
[\[5a.4\]]'dong ngo /_/des pa dga' skyong yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen bkur byed pa thong shig_/dge sbyong mgo reg de la byang chub ga la yod/_byang chub ni mchog tu dka' ba yin no/_/des pa bla ma dge sbyong mgo reg de la byang chub 
[\[5a.5\]]ga la yod/_byang chub ni mchog tu dka' ba'o zhes de skad ma smra zhig_/'on kyang bcom ldan 'das de nyid sangs rgyas yin te/_'dis chos thams cad sangs rgyas so/_/des pa bla ma tshur bdag cag yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen 
[\[5a.6\]]bkur byed du 'dong /_bram ze'i khye'u bla mas lan gnyis lan gsum du yang rdza mkhan dga' skyong la 'di skad ces smras so/_/des pa dga' skyong thong shig/_sangs rgyas 'od srung zhes bya ba nas/_byang chub ni mchog tu dka' ba'o zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma 
[\[5a.7\]]bzhin du'o/_/rdza mkhan dga' skyong gis kyang lan gnyis lan gsum du bram ze'i khye'u bla ma la 'di skad ces smras so/_/des pa bla ma khyod de skad ma smra bar zhes bya ba nas/_bsnyen bkur byed du zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong gis 
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]bram ze'i khye'u bla ma'i shing rta la 'dzegs nas bram ze'i khye'u bla ma'i co to nas bzung ste/_'di skad ces smras so/_/des pa bla ma tshur yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen bkur byed du 'dong /_de nas bram ze'i khye'u bla ma 'di snyam du sems 
[\[5b.2\]]te/_e ma'o sangs rgyas ni mchog ma yin pa ma yin zhing chos bstan pa yang mchog ma yin pa ma yin pas da ltar rdza mkhan dga' skyong yun ring po nas gtum po ma yin/_gzu lums can ma yin/_brlang po ma yin bsri ba med pa ma yin pa des bdag gi skra 
[\[5b.3\]]rnams bzung bar gyur to snyam du rig nas/_rdza mkhan dga' skyong la 'di skad ces smras so/_/des pa dga' skyong de tsam bya ba la thug gam/_des pa de tsam bya ba la thug ces so/_/des pa dga' skyong de lta na thong shig dang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 
[\[5b.4\]]rgyas 'od srung la bsnyen bkur byed du 'dong ngo /_/de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong dang bram ze'i khye'u bla ma shing rta gcig gi nang du zhugs nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba der dong ste/_de gnyis kyis ji tsam bzhon pa'i sa'i bar du bzhon pas 
[\[5b.5\]]song nas bzhon pa las babs te/_rkang pa kho nas kun dga' ra ba'i nang du zhugs te/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba der phyin pa dang /_de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong gis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi zhabs gnyis 
[\[5b.6\]]la mgo bos phyag byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/bram ze'i khye'u bla mas ni bcom ldan 'das dang lhan cig mngon du yang dag par dga' bar 'gyur ba dang /_yang dag par mgu bar 'gyur ba'i gtam rnam pa sna tshogs byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/
[\[5b.7\]]de nas rdza mkhan dga' skyong gis thal mo sbyar ba btud de yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba de logs su 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bram ze'i khye'u bla ma 'di sangs rgyas la mngon par ma dad cing chos dang dge 'dun la mngon par ma dad 
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]lags na/_bcom ldan 'das kyis ji ltar bram ze'i khye'u bla ma 'di sangs rgyas la mngon par dad cing chos dang dge 'dun la mngon par dad par 'gyur ba'i chos de lta bu legs par bshad du gsol/_de nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
[\[6a.2\]]'od srung gis bram ze'i khye'u bla ma la chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par ston par mdzad/_yang dag par 'dzin du 'jug par mdzad/_yang dag par gzengs stod par mdzad/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad de/_rnam grangs du mar chos 
[\[6a.3\]]dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par bstan/_yang dag par 'dzin du bcug_/yang dag par gzengs bstod/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad nas cang mi gsung ngo /_/de nas bram ze'i khye'u bla mas rdza mkhan dga' skyong la 'di skad ces smras 
[\[6a.4\]]so/_/des pa dga' skyong khyod chos 'dul ba 'di lta bu thos na ci'i phyir dad pa yang dag pa kho nas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu mi 'byung /_des pa bla ma kho bo 'di ltar pha ma long ba gnyis gso ba dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung 
[\[6a.5\]]la dus dus su bsod snyoms 'bul ba khyod kyis mi shes sam/_des pa bla ma khyod rab tu byung shig_/kho bo ni re zhig rab tu mi 'byung ngo /_/de nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la rdza mkhan dga' skyong gis 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[6a.6\]]btsun pa bram ze'i khye'u bla ma 'di legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu 'byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos po 'thob pa lta na/_bcom ldan 'das kyis thugs brtse ba nye bar bzung ste/_rab tu 'byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad 
[\[6a.7\]]du gsol/_bram ze'i khye'u bla mas legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu 'byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos po thob par gyur to/_/de nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gis bram ze'i khye'u bla ma rab tu phyung zhing bsnyen par 
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]rdzogs par mdzad nas/_bai b+hi Ting gir ji tsam bzhed kyi bar du bzhugs nas bA rA Na sI gang na ba der rgyu zhing gshegs te rim gyis rgyu zhing gshegs pa dang bA rA Na sIr byon to/_/rgyal po kr-i kIs yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung yul ka shi nas 
[\[6b.2\]]ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs pa dang bA rA Na sIr gshegs te/_bA rA Na sI'i drang srong smra ba [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]kyi nags na bzhugs so zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba der song ste/_phyin pa dang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
[\[6b.3\]]sangs rgyas 'od srung gi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa dang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gis rgyal po kr-i kI la chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par ston par mdzad/_
[\[6b.4\]]yang dag par 'dzin du 'jug par mdzad/_yang dag par gzengs stod par mdzad/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad de/_rnam grangs du mar chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par bstan/_yang dag par 'dzin du bcug_/yang dag par gzengs 
[\[6b.5\]]bstod/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad nas cang mi gsung ngo /_/de nas rgyal po kr-i kI stan las langs te bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gang na ba de 
[\[6b.6\]]logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi dge 'dun dang thabs cig la sang bdag gis gdugs tshod sbyor zhing mchis na gnang bar mdzad du gsol/_yang

[\[16b.6\]]bka' stsal pa/_de lta bas na rjes su gnang ste/_dus sam dus ma yin pa'am/_na ba'am mi na bas/_bu ram la longs spyad par bya ste/_'di la 'gyod par ni mi bya'o/_/tshe dang ldan pa smra 'dod kyi seng ge ro myags pa'i dri mi zhim pas sems rtse gcig pa 
[\[16b.7\]]nyid ma thob nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/_dge slong smra 'dod kyi seng ge ni/_srid pa tha ma yin na ci'i phyir bden pa ma mthong snyam du dgongs na/_lus rul pa'i dri mi zhim pa las gyur par gzigs so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun 
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/_kun dga' bo song la dge slong smra 'dod kyi seng ge'i gtsug lag khang dri dang me tog gi phreng ba dang /_bdug pa dang /_phye ma rnams kyis gtor la gnas mal la yang bdug pas bdug cing me tog dri zhim pos brgyan pa'i 
[\[17a.2\]]me tog gi gdugs bres shig_/btsun pa bka' bzhin 'tshal zhes tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bos bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar mnyan to/_/ji tsam na de bsod snyoms la song ba de'i bar du de'i gtsug lag khang du song nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba bzhin 
[\[17a.3\]]thams cad byas so/_/de nas tshe dang ldan pa smra 'dod kyi seng ge bsod snyoms la brgyus nas gtsug lag khang du 'ongs pa dang lha'i 'byor pa mthong nas/_de nas dga' ba'i yid kyis bsod snyoms la longs spyad de zas kyi bya ba byas nas gtsug lag khang gi phyi rol 
[\[17a.4\]]du rkang pa gnyis bkrus te gtsug lag khang du zhugs nas/_skyil mo krung bcas te lus drang por bsrang nas dran pa mngon du phyogs par der bzhag ste 'dug pa dang /_dri zhim po mnam nas de'i sems mnyam par bzhag par skyes so/_/de nas des nyon mongs pa 
[\[17a.5\]]thams cad spangs te/_dgra bcom pa nyid mngon sum du byas nas zhes bya ba nas/_mngon du smra bar gyur to zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag nga'i dge slong dang nga'i nyan thos 
[\[17a.6\]]sdug pas rnam par grol ba rnams kyi mchog ni 'di lta ste/_dge slong smra 'dod kyi seng ge 'di yin no/_/dge slong rnams the tshom skyes nas the tshom thams cad gcod pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la zhus pa/_btsun pa dge slong gzhan rnams ni mi sdug pas rnam 
[\[17a.7\]]par grol na/_tshe dang ldan pa smra 'dod kyi seng ge sdug pas rnam par grol ba la gzigs/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag 'di ni tshe rabs lnga brgya btud mar sum bcu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams nas shi 'phos shing de nyid du skyes te/_da ltar 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]gyi srid pa tha ma mi rnams su skyes pa na lus rul ba'i dri mnam pas sems rtse gcig pa ma grub pa'o/_/dri zhim po'i dri mnam pa dang /_sems mnyam pa thob nas sdug pas rnam par grol bar gyur to/_/gal te 'di la thabs 'di ma byas par gyur na/_dro bar gyur pa 
[\[17b.2\]]yang 'dis bskyed par mi 'gyur ro/_/de lta bas na rjes su gnang ste/_gzhan gang zhig sdug pa 'di lta bus rnam par grol bar 'gyur ba des rim pa de lta bu bya ste/_'di la 'gyod par ni mi bya'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das yul spong byed nas ljongs rgyu zhing 
[\[17b.3\]]gshegs te yangs pa can du byon nas/_yangs pa can gyi spre'u rdzing gi 'gram na khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas na bzhugs so/_/yangs pa can gyi bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das yul spong byed nas ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs te yangs pa can du 
[\[17b.4\]]byon nas yangs pa can gyi spre'u rdzing gi 'gram na khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas na bzhugs so zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang gcig tu 'dus nas smras pa/_shes ldan dag bcom ldan 'das yul spong byed nas ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs te yangs pa can du byon nas 
[\[17b.5\]]yangs pa can gyi spre'u rdzing gi 'gram na khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas na bzhugs so zhes grag gis/_gal te bdag cag re re nas bcom ldan 'das spyan drangs te gdugs tshod gsol na ni/_bcom ldan 'das slar gshegs dang /_gzhan gyi skabs mi rnyed par 
[\[17b.6\]]'gyur bas de la khrims shig bcas te/_ci nas kyang tshogs shing 'dus pa kho nas bcom ldan 'das gdugs tshod gsol gyi mi gcig pus ni ma yin no zhes de rnams kyis khrims su bca' ba dag byas nas bzhag go/_/de'i tshe yangs pa can gyi bsod nams 
[\[17b.7\]]chen po dang ldan pa bzhi zhig gnas te/_nor can dang /_nor can gyi chung ma dang /_nor can gyi bu dang /_nor can gyi mna' ma'o/_/de dag gi khyim du ni lha dang mi rnams kyi g.yang babs par gyur to/_/de rnams kyis khrims su bcas pa de ma thos par khyim bdag 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]nor can gyis bcom ldan 'das yul spong byed nas ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs te yangs pa can du byon nas/_yangs pa can gyi spre'u rdzing gi 'gram na khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas na bzhugs so zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang yangs pa can nas byung nas

[\[18a.4\]]kyis cang mi gsung bas khyim bdag nor can la gnang ngo /_/de nas khyim bdag nor can gyis bcom ldan 'das cang mi gsung bas gnang bar rig nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan snga nas song ngo /_/
[\[18a.5\]]de nas yangs pa can gyi bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams yangs pa can nas byung nas bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste zhes bya ba nas bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi dge 'dun rnams dang thabs cig tu sang bdag cag gi sdum par gdugs tshod sbyor zhing mchis na gnang bar 
[\[18a.6\]]mdzad du gsol zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_gnas 'jog gi bu dag nga sngar khyim bdag nor can gyis spyan drangs zin to/_/de rnams kyis smras pa/_shes ldan dag khyim bdag nor can gyis ni tshogs kyi

[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]gnangs gdugs tshod gsol lo/_/de nas tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo nang par sngar langs te/_lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs nas/_khyim bdag nor can gyi khyim gang na ba der song ste phyin nas shar sgo nas zhugs pa dang /_ji tsam na gdan ma bshams shing

[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]nas gdan las bzhengs te gshegs so/_/nor can gyi chung mas kyang zhal zas rnams sta gon byas so/_/de nas tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo nang par sngar langs te lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs nas khyim bdag nor can gyi khyim gang na ba der

[\[19b.1\]]ma la chos bshad pa mdzad nas gshegs so/_/nor can gyi bus kyang gtsang zhing bsod pa'i bza' ba dang bca' ba dag sta gon byas so/_/de nas tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo nang par sngar langs te lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te nor can gyi khyim gang na

[\[20a.1\]]khyim bdag rnams bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste lhags pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis yangs pa can gyi bram

[\[20a.7\]]bshams shing zhal zas kyang sta gon ma byas pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang nor can gyi mna' ma la 'di skad ces smras so/_/nor can gyi mna' ma sangs rgyas la sogs pa dge slong gi dge 'dun rnams spyan drangs nas ci snying las chung ngus gnas sam/_des smras 
[\[20b\]]

[\[20b.7\]]bzod par gyur pa dang /_des smras pa/_gal te de lta na nang du zhugs shig de rnams khyim du zhugs pa dang /_gdan bzang po bshams shing zhal zas bsod pa sta gon byas pa yang mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang shin tu ngo mtshar skyes par gyur nas smras pa/_khyim bdag

[\[40b.5\]]yin pa de dag ni nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs te/_dgra bcom pa'i 'bras bu mngon sum du byas so/_tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po'i gang dag yin pa de dag ni lan gcig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o/_/tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu'i gang dag yin pa de dag ni rgyun du zhugs

[\[40b.7\]]'phrul che ba rnams kyi mchog tu bstan lags na/_'on kyang tshe dang ldan pa bram ze ka pi nas gdams ngag phog pa gang dag lags pa de dag ni dgra bcom pa/_tshe dang ldan pa maud gal gyi bu chen po'i gang dag lags pa de dag ni lan gcig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu/_tshe dang 
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]ldan pa shA ri'i bu'i gang dag lags pa de dag ni rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu mngon sum du bgyis pa la gzigs/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag da ltar du ma zad ji ltar 'das pa'i dus na yang ka pi na'i gdams ngag phog pa gang 
[\[41a.2\]]dag yin pa de dag ni gzugs med pa'i khams la rab tu bkod/_maud gal gyi bu'i gang dag yin pa de dag ni gzugs kyi khams dang /_shA ri'i bu'i gang dag yin pa de dag ni mngon par shes pa lnga la rab tu bkod pa de nyon cig_/dge slong dag sngon byung ba dgon pa'i gnas 
[\[41a.3\]]zhig na drang srong gnyis shig gnas te so sor 'khor lnga brgya lnga brgya yod do/_/ji tsam dus gzhan zhig na de gnyis las gcig shi nas de'i bram ze'i khye'u rnams bla ma dang bral ba'i sdug bsngal ba dang /_yid mi bde bas gdungs nas phan tshun 'phyan 'phyan nas drang srong 
[\[41a.4\]]cig shos de'i drung du 'ongs pa dang /_des de rnams mig mchi mas gang ba mthong nas dris pa/_bram ze'i khye'u dag khyed kyi mkhan po de gar song /_gum mo/_/des bsams pa/_bdag 'das na bram ze'i khye'u rnams 'di 'dra ba'i gnas skabs su 'gyur gyis/_ma la 
[\[41a.5\]]bdag gis 'di dag bsdu bar bya'o snyam nas des de rnams dbugs phyung ste bsdus so/_/ji tsam dus gzhan zhig na drang srong de yang nad kyis thebs par gyur nas/_de'i slob ma mchog gsum yod pa la des gcig la ni lnga brgya/_gnyis pa la ni nyis brgya lnga bcu/_gsum pa 
[\[41a.6\]]la ni nyis brgya lnga bcu gtad do/_/de ni 'chi ba'i chos dang ldan par gyur to/_/de la lnga brgya gtad pa gang yin pa des ni ji ltar gzugs med pa'i khams la bkod pa de ltar gdams ngag phog go/_/nyis brgya lnga bcu gtad pa gang yin pa des ni ji ltar gzugs kyi khams la bkod 
[\[41a.7\]]pa de ltar gdams ngag phog go/_/nyis brgya lnga bcu phog pa cig shos kyis ni ji ltar mngon par shes pa lnga la bkod pa de ltar gdams ngag phog go/_/dge slong dag ji snyam du sems/_de'i tshe de'i dus kyi drang srong bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya gzugs med pa'i khams la 'god pa gang 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]yin pa de ni dge slong ka pi na 'di nyid yin no/_/nyis brgya lnga bcu gzugs kyi khams la rab tu 'god pa gang yin pa de ni dge slong maud gal gyi bu 'di nyid yin no/_/cig shos kyis nyis brgya lnga bcu mngon par shes pa lnga la rab tu dgod pa gang yin pa de ni dge slong shA ri'i bu 'di nyid yin 
[\[41b.2\]]no/_/'on kyang dge slong ka pi nas gdams ngag phog pa gang yin pa de ni dbang po rnon po yin no/_/maud gal gyi bu'i gang yin pa de dag ni dbang po 'bring po yin no/_/shA ri'i bu'i gang yin pa de dag ni dbang po rtul po yin no/_/gal te shA ri'i bus gdams ngag phog par ma gyur na de 
[\[41b.3\]]dag gis dro bar gyur pa yang rtogs par mi 'gyur ro/_/ke na'i bu ni ral pa can/_/ji ltar sangs rgyas bsngags brjod pa/_/rab tu dang ba'i sems kyis ni/_/thams cad mthun par mnyan par gyis/_/ke na'i bu yi khyim du ni/_/dus gcig cig na ston pa po/_/dpa' chen nyan

[\[41b.6\]]gyur te/_/sangs rgyas de ci ke na'i bu/_/dris pa de ni bdag la smros/_/shAkya'i rigs las skyes pa yi/_/ston pa gang zag zla med yod/_/de ni chos kun blo ldan pas/_/de bas sangs rgyas zhes bya'o/_/'dus byas 'jig par 'gyur ba'i chos/_/'das pa sangs rgyas kyis

[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]shes byas/_/bsgom par bya ba rnams kyang bsgoms/_/spang bar bya ba spangs pas na/_/de bas sangs rgyas zhes bya'o/_/gang zhig bltams par gyur pa na/_/rgya mtsho ri bor bcas pa yi/_/sa 'di rab tu g.yos mdzad pa/_/de la thal sbyar phyag 'tshal lo/_/

[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]pa dang ni ma grags pas/_/chu yis pad ma bzhin ma gos/_/srog gcod pa ni rnam spangs te/_/ma byin pa la dga' mi byed/_/bden pa gsung zhing tshangs par spyod/_/de ni phra ma spangs pa yin/_/tshig rtsub dag ni mi gsung zhing /_/dus su bab pa 'jam por gsung /_/

[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]bcom nas ni/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub dam pa thob/_/bram ze nga'i dran 'khor lo ste/_/nga yi shes rab blon po yin/_/brtson 'grus shugs mgyogs rta pho ni/_/btang na dang la 'byung ba yin/_/nga yi nor bu dam pa ni/_/ting 'dzin mun pa gsal byed yin/_/btang

[\[44b.3\]]des/_/long bar gyur cing gnyid log pa'i/_/skye dgu 'di dag sad par bya/_/phyogs rnams kun nas nga rgyal bas/_/nga la sdo ba'i dgra yod min/_/nyon mongs tshogs rnams 'joms pa yi/_/'khor bzhi po dag nga yi sras/_/rdzu 'phrul kun dga' phun sum tshogs/_/lam 
[\[44b.4\]]gyi bzhi mdor sprul pa byas/_/mdo sde skyes pa'i rabs dag bkram/_/skyes bu chen po rnams kyis sten/_/rnam thar gsum gyi sgo mo can/_/dran pa'i srung ma rnams kyis bsrungs/_/ngo tsha khrel yod kyis g.yogs pa'i/_/grong khyer sngon gyi sangs rgyas kyis/_/bsten 
[\[44b.5\]]pa dag tu de bzhin gshegs/_/rgyal po nga ni bzhugs nas su/_/nga yi chos kyi g.yul sprad de/_/chos kyi rnga bo che brdungs nas/_/bdud rnams mtha' dag bcom byas te/_/nga ni byang chub phyir dbang bskur/_/tshad med pa dag rab bsgoms pa/_/nga yi rgyan du bdog pa

[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]sgrib pa 'joms pa yi/_/dbang po lnga yi dong ba dang /_/gang gi nyon mongs thag 'jud pa'i/_/yang dag spong ba bzhi po yis/_/ngas ni dpa' bo g.yul sprad de/_/chos kyi rnga bo che brdungs nas/_/bdud dpung bcas par bcom byas te/_/nga ni byang chub dbang

[\[59a.1\]]bcu gcig pa/_rgyal po gzugs can snying po gzhan gyi bud med la lhag par chags pas glang po che la zhon nas grong khyer gyi srang rnams su song nas mig phan tshun du lta zhing 'khyam par byed do/_/de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab na tshong dpon phyug pa nor mang ba longs spyod che ba

[\[63a.1\]]nas des dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa bsdus te btang ba las des mtha' 'khob pa skya bseng de rnams phab nas gte'u bzung dpya blangs te slar bde bar 'ongs nas khyer te 'ongs pa de bzhin du rgyal po la phul ba dang /_de shin tu dga' nas de nyid la rjes su yi rangs

[\[67b.1\]]gzigs par mi 'gyur ro/_/de nas des bstan nas khyim du khrid nas bzhag go/_/de nas 'tsho byed sha'i don du bsil ba'i tshal gyi dur khrod du song ba las bsil ba'i tshal gyi dur khrod ni shi ba'i ros stong pa ma yin pas de nas ro'i sha blangs te yo byad kyi khyad par dag dang g.yos byas nas

[\[67b.3\]]rngams nas smras pa/_gal te de lta na ci'i phyir gsad ces mi bzod pa skyes pas de'i skran rdol nas yar slon mar 'khrus te/_kha nas khrag dang bcas par byung ba de mthong nas brgyal te sa la 'gyel to/_/de nas chu gtor nas dbugs slar byung ba dang khrus byas te 'phrod pa'i

[\[77a.2\]]su phul ba dang /_de'i mgo gang yin mjug gang yin pa ma shes nas rgyal pos blon po rnams la bsgo ba ji lta bu yin rtogs shig_/de rnams kyis nyin thog thag dpyad na ma shes nas dgongs ka khyim du dong ngo /_/sa ga mas zhes bya ba nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]'dzin gyis dris pa zhes bya

[\[78b.1\]]byin cig_/dpal skyed kyis smras pa/_bzang mo bdag cag la sa ga mas phan btags kyis byin cig_/gal te de lta na sbyin par bya'o/_/de nas [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]'dzin gyis dpal gyi 'byor pa chen pos bag mar blangs nas/_de de'i rim gro byed par gyur te sa ga ma ni de ltar mi byed do/_/

[\[86b.1\]]so/_/don gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na nad g.yog la sbyin pa sbyin/_btsun pa dge slong bro g.yog bgyid pa rang gi ched du bsod snyoms tshol du mchis na bro 'tshal ba'i bgyi ba nyams par 'gyur bas des bdag gi bsod snyoms la longs spyad na 'grub par 'gyur lags so/_/sa 
[\[86b.2\]]ga ma de yang thugs su chud de legs so legs so/_/don gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na dge 'dun la nas thug nar ma sbyin/_btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan thos rnams la drod chung ba yang mchis/_drod che ba yang mchis te/_de la re zhig drod chung ba gang lags pa de rnams 
[\[86b.3\]]ni nas thug 'tshal na drod skye bar 'gyur la/_drod che ba gang lags pa de rnams ni nyam skye bar 'gyur lags so/_/sa ga ma de yang thugs su chud de legs so legs so/_/don gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na dge 'dun la sman nar ma sbyin/_btsun pa bcom ldan 'das kyi nyan 
[\[86b.4\]]thos rnams las bro nad mang ba dang bro nad nyung ba mchis te/_de la bro nad mang po gang lags pa de rnams la ni re zhig 'tshal ches/_bro nad nyung ba gang lags pa de rnams kyis longs spyad na lhag par yang bde ba la reg par gnas par 'gyur lags so/_/sa ga ma de yang 
[\[86b.5\]]thugs su chud de legs so legs so/_/don gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na/_dge slong rnams la dbyar gyi gos ras chen sbyin/_btsun pa de ring kho na bdag gis bu mo mngag gzhug bgyid pa dus tshod gsol bar btang ba las/_des dge slong rnams sgren mor khrus 
[\[86b.6\]]byas pa mthong nas/_de slar mchis te bdag la 'phags pa rnams ni bzhud la 'tsho ba pa nal phrug rnams kyis gtsug lag khang du btab bo zhes mchi bas de rnams dbyar gyi gos ras chen gyis bkab par 'gyur lags so/_/sa ga ma de yang thugs su chud de legs 
[\[86b.7\]]so legs so/_/don gyi dbang ci zhig mthong na dge slong ma rnams la khrus ras sbyin/_btsun pa dus 'di gcig na khyim pa mo rab tu mang po dag dge slong ma rab tu mang po rnams chu bo gnas ldan la sgren mor khrus bgyid pa las khyim pa mo de rnams kyis bgad cing 
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]bgad nas mdzub ker gyis dge slong ma rnams kyi mdoms nye bar ston te/_de dag gis nye bar bstan pa rnams bag 'khums par gyur pas de rnams bdag gi khrus ras kyis bkab nas khrus bgyid par 'gyur lags so/_/sa ga ma legs so legs 
[\[87a.2\]]so/_/khyod kyis bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po brgyad smras pa gang yin pa de dag ni rdzas las byung ba'i bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po bdun rnams la yang dag par snang zhing 'di dag ni rdzas las byung ba'i bsod nams bya ba'i dngos po bdun po gang dag la bsod nams 
[\[87a.3\]]sam/_bsod nams kyi 'bras bu'am/_bsod nams kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa 'di tsam mo zhes tshad gzung bar mi nus kyi 'on kyang bsod nams mang ba'i phyir bsod nams kyi phung po chen po zhes bya ba'i grangs su 'gro ba rnams kyi nang du 'dus so/_/btsun pa 
[\[87a.4\]]bdag gis 'di skad ces bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong che ge mo de snga nas dge slong 'di yang kun tu sbyor ba gsum spangs nas rgyun du zhugs pa rnam par mi ltung ba'i chos can rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu nges par gzhol ba/_re ltar thogs na srid pa lan bdun par 'gyur te/_
[\[87a.5\]]lha dang mi rnams kyi nang du lan bdun du nying mtshams sbyor zhing 'khor nas sdug bsngal gyi mthar byed par 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan pa thos te/_de mnyan yod du res 'ga' mchi zhing slar yang mchi bar 'gyur la/_des bdag gi glo bur du mchi ba'i zas zhes bya ba nas/_dbyar 
[\[87a.6\]]gyi gos ras chen dang /_dge slong mas khrus ras longs spyod par 'gyur lags so zhes bya ba'i bar de thos nas kyang dga' ba dang rab tu dga' ba rgya chen po dge ba nges par 'byung ba dang ldan pa thob par 'gyur lags so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong che ge mo de dge slong 
[\[87a.7\]]'di ni kun tu sbyor ba gsum spangs nas 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug bsrabs pa'i phyir lan gcig phyir 'ong bar 'gyur te/_'jig rten 'dir lan gcig 'ongs nas sdug bsngal gyi mthar byed par 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan pa de yang mnyan yod du res 'ga' 
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]mchi zhing slar mchi la/_des bdag gi glo bur ba'i zas zhes bya ba nas dbyar gyi gos ras chen dang /_dge slong mas khrus ras zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin te/_thos nas kyang dga' ba dang rab tu dga' ba rgya chen po dge ba nges par 'byung ba dang ldan pa thob par 'gyur lags 
[\[87b.2\]]so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong che ge mo de dge slong 'di ni tha ma'i cha dang mthun pa'i kun tu sbyor ba lnga spangs pas phyir mi 'ong bar 'gyur te/_'jig rten 'dir phyir mi 'ong bar 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan pa de yang res 'ga' mnyan yod du mchi zhing slar mchi la/_des 
[\[87b.3\]]bdag gi glo bur ba'i zas zhes bya ba nas dbyar gyi gos ras chen dang /_dge slong mas khrus ras zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du ste/_thos nas kyang dga' ba dang rab tu dga' ba rgya chen po dge ba nges par 'byung ba dang ldan pa thob par 'gyur lags so/_/bcom ldan 
[\[87b.4\]]'das kyis dge slong che ge mo de dge slong 'di ni nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs te dgra bcom pa mngon sum du byas nas phung po lhag ma med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su 'jug par 'gyur ro zhes lung bstan pa de yang res 'ga' mnyan yod du mchi zhing slar yang 
[\[87b.5\]]mchi la/_des bdag gi glo bur ba'i zas zhes bya ba nas dbyar gyi gos ras chen dang /_dge slong mas khrus ras zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du ste/_thos nas kyang dga' ba dang rab tu dga' ba rgya chen po dge ba nges par 'byung ba dang ldan pa thob par 'gyur lags 
[\[87b.6\]]so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]'dzin gyi ma sa ga ma la chos dang ldan pa'i gtam gyis yang dag par bstan yang dag par 'dzin du bcug_/yang dag par gzengs bstod/_yang dag par rab tu dga' bar mdzad nas gdan las bzhengs te gshegs so/_/
[\[87b.7\]]de nas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod du ji tsam bzhed kyi bar du bzhugs nas yangs pa can gang na ba der rgyu zhing gshegs te/_rim gyis rgyu zhing gshegs pa na yangs pa can du byon nas yangs pa can gyi spre'u rdzing gi 'gram gyi khang pa brtsegs pa na bzhugs so/_/de nas 
[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]bcom ldan 'das snga dro na bza' sham thabs mnabs chos gos dang lhung bzed bsnams nas yangs pa can du bsod snyoms la zhugs so/_/de'i tshe dge slong rab tu mang po kun dga' ra ba'i sgo na gnas mal mi gtsang bas gos pa rnams 'khru ba dang /_skems 
[\[88a.2\]]pa dang /_'dings par byed do/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rab tu mang po kun dga' ra ba'i sgo na gnas mal mi gtsang bas gos pa rnams 'khru ba dang /_skems pa dang /_'dings par byed pa gzigs so/_/gzigs nas kyang yang 'di snyam du dgongs te/_
[\[88a.3\]]bram ze dang khyim bdag dad pa can dag pags pa dang /_sha dang /_khrag zad zad du sbyin pa btang zhing bsod nams byas pa gang yin pa de la dge slong rnams bag med cing ma brtags par spyod par byed do snyam du dgongs nas yangs pa can du bsod snyoms spyad de/_zhal 
[\[88a.4\]]zas kyi bya ba mdzad nas zas phyi ma'i bsod snyoms spangs shing lhung bzed dang na bza' chos gos mkhos su phab nas zhabs bsil te nang du yang dag 'jog la gtsug lag khang du zhugs so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das dgongs ka nang du yang dag 'jog las bzhengs nas dge 
[\[88a.5\]]slong gi dge 'dun gyi gung la gdan bshams pa nyid la bzhugs so/_/bzhugs nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag 'dir nga snga dro sham thabs bgos lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te yangs pa can du bsod snyoms la 
[\[88a.6\]]zhugs pa las ngas dge slong rab tu mang po dag kun dga' ra ba'i sgo na gnas mal mi gtsang bas gos pa rnams 'khru ba dang /_skems pa dang /_'dings par byed pa mthong ngo /_/mthong nas kyang 'di snyam du dgongs te/_bram ze dang khyim bdag dad pa can dag pags 
[\[88a.7\]]pa dang /_sha dang khrag zad zad du sbyin pa btang zhing bsod nams byas pa gang yin pa de la dge slong rnams bag med cing ma brtags par spyod par byed do/_/dge slong dag dad pas byin pa la bag yod pas spyod pa dang /_dus su spyod pa dang /_skabs skabs su spyod 
[\[88b\]]
[\[88b.1\]]pa dang /_brtags te spyod pa dang /_cung zad spyod pa ni legs so legs so/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dad pas byin pa la bag yod pas spyad par bya ba'i bsngags pa gsungs nas dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag de lta bas na dge 'dun gyi gnas mal la gding

[\[88b.4\]]khyod kyis mi rgyab nag po pha bi mthong ngam/_btsun pa mthong lags so/_/kun dga' bo mi pha bi ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi gsung rab la rab tu byung bar gyur te/_de nas 'dis dge 'dun gyi gnas mal la ming tsam gyi gding ba dang /_dri ma can gyi 
[\[88b.5\]]gding ba dang /_shur bu dang /_rgya cang dang /_lcug gu'i gding bas bting nas longs spyad pa de'i las kyi rnam par smin pas tshe rabs lnga brgyar rgyab nag por gyur to/_/da dung yang rgyab nag por gyur to zhes rig par mdzad nas yangs pa can du bsod snyoms mdzad do zhes bya ba 
[\[88b.6\]]nas nang du yang dag 'jog las bzhengs nas zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi gung la gdan bshams pa nyid la bzhugs so/_/bzhugs nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag de ring nga snga dro sham 
[\[88b.7\]]thabs bgos lhung bzed dang chos gos thogs te yangs pa can du bsod snyoms la zhugs pa las/_de na ngas mi rgyab nag por 'dug pa zhig mthong ste/_de ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi gsung rab la dge slong du gyur pa las de na des dge 'dun gyi 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]gnas mal la ming tsam gyi gding ba dang zhes bya ba nas tshe rabs lnga brgya'i bar du rgyab nag por gyur to zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/dge slong dag de lta bas na dge slong rnams kyis deng phyin chad ming tsam gyi gding ba dang /_dri ma can gyi gding bas dge 
[\[89a.2\]]'dun gyi gnas mal la longs spyad par mi bya'o/_/longs spyad na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/'on kyang gding ba gnyis kyis longs spyad par bya ste/_stug na ni rim gcig gis so/_/seng ba zhig na rim gnyis kyis so/_/dge slong rnams gding ba bkra zhing bkra ba kha 
[\[89a.3\]]tshar ma bcad pa dag 'chang bar byed pa bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas smod par byed/_gzhogs 'phyas byed/_kha zer bar byed nas bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_sngon po'am/_'dam ka'am/_kha bsgyur ba'i gding ba gris dras pa bcang bar bya ste/_
[\[89a.4\]]gzhan du na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/dge slong zhig g.yan pa'i nad kyis nyen pas de'i chos gos rnams rnag dang khrag gis reg nas dri mi zhim la sbrang ma rnams 'du zhing de bsod snyoms la zhugs pa de bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas smod par byed gzhogs 
[\[89a.5\]]'phyas byed/_kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de lta bas na rjes su gnang ste g.yan pa dgab pa bcang bar bya ste/_zhag lnga'am/_drug lon nas bkru bar bya'o/_/gzhan 
[\[89a.6\]]du na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/dge slong mdze'i nad kyis nyen pa dag dge 'dun gyi gnas mal dang /_khyams dang /_rdzing dang /_sgo khang dang /_bang rim dang /_'chag sa rnams dang /_shing drung gi gnas rnams na dri mi zhim pa dang /_sbrang ma rnams 'khor bas 
[\[89a.7\]]gnas shing longs spyad par byed pa de dag bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas smod par byed/_gzhogs 'phyas byed/_kha zer bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]slong dag dge slong mdze nad kyis thebs pa rnams kyi phyir ngas kun tu spyod pa'i chos dag bca' bar bya ste/_dge slong mdze nad kyis thebs pas dge 'dun gyi gnas mal dang /_gnas khang la longs spyad par mi bya/_khyams rnams dang /_ji skad du bsgrags pa'i khyams la

[\[89b.4\]]lags sam/_nye ba 'khor mi rung ngo /_/btsun pa mon dar gyi chos gos la chos gos gsum du byin gyis brlabs nas chos gos lhag par byin gyis brlabs te bcang du rung lags sam/_nye ba 'khor ci dgar zad de/_de bzhin du 'dod pa bzhin du bal dang sha na'i ras la yang 
[\[89b.5\]]rung ngo //_//sdom la/_gso ma skra yi chos gos dang /_/gcer bu skra 'bal lo ma'i gos/_/mthang gos can dang g.yang gzhi dang /_/gso ras seng ras pa char sngo/_/gleng gzhi ni mnyan yod na'o/_/dge slong zhig bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der song ste phyin pa dang 
[\[89b.6\]]bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la mgo bos phyag byas te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/_/phyogs gcig tu 'dug nas bcom ldan 'das la dge slong des 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag gso ma'i chos gos 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis

[\[90a.1\]]'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag skra'i chos gos bcang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_skyes bu gti mug 'di ni mu stegs pa'i rtags so zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar

[\[90a.3\]]yin no/_/gzhan yang ngas chos gos gsum rjes su gnang na ci'i phyir khyod gcer bu byed/_de lta bas na dge slong gis gcer bu mi bya'o/_/byas na nyes pa sbom por 'gyur ro/_/de nas dge slong de gcer bu ma gnang ba dang bslab pa rnams spangs te nyams

[\[90a.6\]]yul phyogs sna tshogs na gnas pa'i bya rnams kyis thos nas 'dus par gyur te so so nas bsams pa/_de'i khyim thab tu bdag 'gyur ro snyam mo/_/des rma bya zhig mthong nas des smras pa/_'di ni bdag gi khyim thab tu 'gyur ro/_/de la gzhan dag gis 'dis khyod 
[\[90a.7\]]khyim thab tu bdams so zhes smras pa dang /_des mjug ma bsgrengs nas gar byed par brtsams pa de yul 'khor skyong gis mthong nas smras pa/_'di ci zhig gi phyir gar byed/_gzhan dag gis smras pa/_khyod kyi bu mo'i khyim thab tu gyur pas so/_/des smras pa/_ngo tsha dang 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]khrel dang mi ldan pas 'di la bdag gi bu mo mi sbyin no zhes pa rma bya des thos nas yul 'khor skyong gi drung du song ste tshigs su bcad pas smras pa/_dbyangs snyan yid 'ong kha dog mdzes pa dang /_/'dab ma 'dom gang tshad ldan mgrin pa ni/_/nor bu bai DUr+ya dang 'dra gyur na/_/ci slad khyod kyi

[\[90b.4\]]ngo /_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag skra 'bal bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_ngas 'dreg par rjes su gnang na ci'i phyir khyod skra 'bal/_skyes bu gti mug 'di lta ste/_skra 'bal ba 'di 
[\[90b.5\]]ni mu stegs pa'i brtul zhugs yin no/_/de lta bas na dge slong gis skra 'bal bar mi bya'o/_/'bal na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag lo ma'i bgo ba 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/
[\[90b.6\]]bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_skyes bu gti mug 'di ni mu stegs pa'i brtul zhugs yin no zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun

[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]gos dang bcas pa'i chos gos kyis 'tsho bar mi bya'o/_/'tsho bar byed na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag g.yang gzhi 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das

[\[91a.3\]]chos gos 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'di ni mu stegs kyi rgyal mtshan yin no zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/
[\[91a.4\]]btsun pa bdag pa char 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_skyes bu gti mug 'di lta ste/_pa char 'di ni khyim pa'i chos yin te zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/yang dge slong zhig 
[\[91a.5\]]gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag chos gos thams cad sngon por 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_'di ni khyim pa 'chang bar byed do/_/de lta bas na dge slong gis chos gos thams cad sngon por bcang

[\[91b.1\]]kyis bka' stsal pa/_'di ni mu stegs pa'i rtags yin no/_/zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag kha tshar gdengs ka lta bu'i

[\[91b.7\]]no/_/zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/yang dge slong zhig gis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to/_/btsun pa bdag rnga bal gyi klam 'chang bar 'tshal lo/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_skyes bu 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]gti mug 'di ni khyim pa'i rtags yin no zhes bya ba nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i bar gong ma bzhin du'o/_/tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' mchin nad can dge slong med pa'i gnas sgo 'phar med pa zhig tu dbyar gnas par dam bcas te snam sbyar med par 
[\[92a.2\]]de phyi rol du byung ba dang /_de'i snam sbyar rkun pos khyer ro zhes bya ba'i bar du ste/_de'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong gis dge slong med pa'i gnas sgo 'phar med par dbyar gnas par dam mi bca' zhing snam sbyar med par gar du yang 'gro bar mi bya'o/_/

[\[92a.6\]]kyi chos gos kyi rnyed pa sbyin par bya'o zhes gsungs na/_dge slong rnams kyis dag gi dbye byas pa'i mod kho na la ljongs rgyur dang ba dang de rnams kyi rnyed pa sus kyang ma blangs nas/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de'i rnyed pa len du gzhug pa'i phyir dge slong la

[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]ro/_/bcol ba'i dge slong gzhan dag gis ma blangs nas/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de rnams kyis thams cad mnod par bya'o/_/dge tshul rnams dong nas sus kyang rnyed pa ma blangs so/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_de rnams kyi mkhan

[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]de nang par sngar langs te/_rgan zhugs de khrid nas rings pa rings par song ste/_rim gyis ri 'or du phyin pa dang /_de nas gtsug lag khang na gnas pa'i rgan zhugs cig shos des 'ongs pa legs so zhes kun dga' bar gyur pa'i tshig smras nas de gnyis ngal sos pa

[\[95a.1\]]shig_/cig shos kyis smras pa/_bdag mi bgod do/_/ci'i phyir bdag la nyes pa byung na mi rung ngo /_/des kyang smras pa/_gal te de lta na bdag la yang nyes pa 'di nyid yin no/_/de gnyis sems khong du chud cing 'dug pa las/_ji tsam na wa bzhin rgyas 
[\[95a.2\]]zhes bya ba de gnyis kyi drung du 'ongs nas des smras pa tsha bo dag ci ste sems khong du chud cing 'dug_/zhang po bdag cag ni nya rnyed pas so/_/ci ste mi 'gyed/_zhang po nyes pas 'jigs so/_/khyod ni nyes pas 'jigs pa bzhin rgyas ni rkang pa spyi'u tshugs su sems can dmyal

[\[95a.6\]]ste 'khang bar mi bya'o/_/de des gnad la phab pas skyengs nas cang mi smra bar 'dug go/_/de nas cig shos kyi drung du song nas smras pa/_tsha bo khyod kyis 'dir ci dag byas/_zhang po bdag gis skam la song ste bdag gis skam la thon pa'i nya rnams bsad do/_/tsha bo gal

[\[95b\]]
[\[95b.1\]]do/_/gzhan yang gal te des ma bskrogs na khyod kyis ji ltar gsad/_mdor na 'dir nya rnyed pa gang yin pa de rnams ni de'i mthu yin pas des ci byin pa de blang bar bya ste 'khang bar mi bya'o/_/de des gnad la phab pas skyengs nas cang mi smra bar 'dug go/_/bzhin rgyas

[\[95b.5\]]pa'i/_/nya bo che zhig thogs nas ni/_/shin tu don grub ga las 'ongs/_/des smras pa/_gang phyir blun po rtsod gyur cing /_/chos dang chos min mi shes pa'i/_/rgyal po'i mdzod ni 'phel ba ltar/_/des ni chog shes 'tsho bar 'gyur/_/des kyang tshigs su bcad pas smras pa/_

[\[95b.7\]]rgan zhugs 'di gnyis yin te/_de na yang de gnyis 'dis bslus la da ltar yang de gnyis bslus so/_/gleng gzhi ni mnyan yod na'o/_/ri 'or zhig na khyim bdag cig yod pa des gtsug lag khang zhig brtsigs nas der dge slong brgya dbyar gnas par dam bcas so/_/
[\[96a\]]
[\[96a.1\]]khyim bdag de'i blo skyes pa/_ras yug brgya bsgrubs te dge slong gi dge 'dun gdugs tshod gsol nas/_dge slong re re la yang ras yug re re bskon no snyam mo/_/ji tsam na nye [{]dga'as [,]dgas [}]khyim bdag che ge mo zhig gis dge slong gi dge 'dun gdugs tshod gsol te/_re re 
[\[96a.2\]]la yang ras yug re re bskon no zhes thos so/_/thos nas kyang bsams pa/_gal te de las ras yug gcig ma grub na bdag nye dga' ma yin no snyam nas de mgron du ma bos bzhin du song ngo /_/khyim bdag gis dge slong gi dge 'dun gdugs tshod gsol nas ras rnams

[\[114b.7\]]bka' stsal pa/_mtshams kyi nang la mtshams kyi nang du 'du shes te shi ba'i yo byad 'ged par byed na ma bgos pa nyes par bgos pa yin te/_slar yang mtshams na 'dug pa rnams dang lhan cig bgo bar bya ste/_gzhan du na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du

[\[115a.1\]]mtshams kyi nang la yid gnyis kyis 'ged par byed na ma bgos pa nyes par bgos pa yin te/_slar yang mtshams na 'dug pa rnams dang lhan cig bgo bar bya ste/_gzhan du na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro/_/mtshams kyi phyi rol la mtshams kyi nang gi 'du shes

[\[115a.4\]]bcom ldan 'das la dge slong rnams kyis gsol pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_dge slong dag dge slong des yid gcugs par bya ba ma yin pa la yid gcugs par bskyed de/_'di ltar gang gis bskur ba de la yid gcugs pas spyad do/_/'on kyang dge slong 
[\[115a.5\]]gis dge slong la dge slong gi lag nas chos gos dag bskur ba na gang la bskur ba de la yid gcugs pas spyad na legs par spyad pa yin no/_/gang gis bskur ba de la yid gcugs pas spyad na nyes par spyad pa yin no/_/dge slong gis dge slong la chos gos dag skur bar byed na 
[\[115a.6\]]gang la bskur ba de ni shi la gang gis bskur ba de la yid gcugs pas spyad na nyes par spyad pa yin no/_/gang la bskur ba de'i shi ba'i yo byad du rung bar byin gyis rlob par byed na legs par byin gyis brlabs pa yin no/_/dge slong gis dge slong la chos gos dag skur bar byed pa 
[\[115a.7\]]na gang gis bskur ba de ni shi la gang gis bskur ba de'i shi ba'i yo byad du rung bar byin gyis rlob par byed na nyes par byin gyis brlabs pa yin no/_/dge slong gis dge slong la chos gos dag skur bar byed pa na/_gang la bskur ba des slar bzlog la/_gang gis bskur ba de la yid 
[\[115b\]]
[\[115b.1\]]gcugs pas spyad na legs par spyad pa yin no/_/gang la bskur ba de la yid gcugs pas spyad na nyes par spyad pa yin no/_/dge slong gis dge slong la chos gos dag skur ba na gang la bskur ba de ni shi la gang la bskur ba de'i shi ba'i yo byad du rung bar byin gyis rlob par byed na
